{"result":"`nvfetcher` does not explicitly support Go modules (like `vendorHash` or `goModules`) . There is no direct equivalent to `cargo_lock` for Go packages within `nvfetcher`'s current functionality . The tool primarily focuses on Rust's `Cargo.lock` for dependency hashing .\n\n### Rust `cargo_lock` Support\n\n`nvfetcher` provides specific support for Rust's `Cargo.lock` files . This feature allows `nvfetcher` to extract `outputHashes` for Git dependencies listed in `Cargo.lock` files, which is used by `rustPlatform.buildRustPackage` in Nixpkgs .\n\nTo enable this, you can specify `cargo_locks` in your `nvfetcher.toml` configuration . For example:\n```toml\n[rust-git-dependency-example]\nsrc.manual = \"8a5f37a8f80a3b05290707febf57e88661cee442\"\nfetch.git = \"https://gist.github.com/NickCao/6c4dbc4e15db5da107de6cdb89578375\"\ncargo_locks = [\"Cargo.lock\"]\n```\n\n\nThe `cargo_locks` option takes a list of file paths relative to the source root . `nvfetcher` will then extract these lock files and calculate the `outputHashes` for Git dependencies . This process is handled by the `fetchRustGitDepsRule`  , which parses the `Cargo.lock` file and prefetches the Git dependencies to obtain their SHA256 hashes . The resulting `outputHashes` are then included in the generated Nix expression .\n\n### Automatic Hash Updates\n\nFor Rust packages, the `fetchRustGitDeps` function automatically updates the dependency hashes . When `nvfetcher` runs, it will:\n1. Extract the specified `Cargo.lock` file(s) .\n2. Parse the `Cargo.lock` to identify Git dependencies .\n3. For each Git dependency, it will prefetch the source using `prefetch` and extract the SHA256 hash .\n4. These hashes are then mapped to the package name and version, and included in the `generated.nix` output  .\n\nThis mechanism ensures that the `outputHashes` for Rust Git dependencies are automatically kept up-to-date .\n\n## Notes\n\nThe codebase shows no direct support for Go modules or an equivalent to `cargo_lock` for Go packages. The `cargo_locks` functionality is specifically designed for Rust's `Cargo.lock` files and their Git dependencies . While `nvfetcher` is extensible, adding support for Go modules would require implementing new rules and parsing logic similar to how `FetchRustGitDepsQ` and `fetchRustGitDepsRule` are handled for Rust .\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Getting Started (berberman/nvfetcher)](/wiki/berberman/nvfetcher#2)\n- [Architecture (berberman/nvfetcher)](/wiki/berberman/nvfetcher#5)\n- [Development (berberman/nvfetcher)](/wiki/berberman/nvfetcher#6)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.REDACTED\n"}
```

Here are the results of the search. Let me summarize what was found across the different sources:

## Nix `buildGoModule` vendorHash & Alternatives

### The `vendorHash` Problem
`buildGoModule` builds Go programs managed with Go modules through a two-phase build: an intermediate fetcher derivation called `goModules` fetches all dependencies, and a final derivation uses the output to build binaries. The `vendorHash` is the hash of the output of this intermediate fetcher derivation.

The common workflow pain point is that when updating a Go module, finding out the `vendorSha256`/`vendorHash` ahead of time is not straightforward — you typically try to install with a wrong hash and then fill in the expected one. To obtain the actual hash, you set `vendorHash = lib.fakeSha256;` and run the build.

### `gomod2nix` Approach
The `gomod2nix` project provides tooling to integrate Go modules with Nix, allowing for reproducible builds of Go applications within the Nix ecosystem. It uses a `gomod2nix.toml` file as an intermediate representation that bridges Go's dependency information and Nix — this file is generated by the CLI and consumed by Nix functions. Both files should be tracked in version control to ensure reproducible builds.

However, gomod2nix's builder depends on its CLI tool, which in turn depends on the builder — complicating NUR integration and bootstrapping.

### `go-overlay` (purpleclay) — A Newer Alternative
Compared to gomod2nix and nixpkgs' `buildGoModule`, `go-overlay` offers 100+ Go versions (1.17 to latest), availability of new releases up to 4 hours, support for release candidates, no `vendorHash` required, unpatched Go binaries, and Go workspace support.

Neither `buildGoModule` nor `gomod2nix` support workspaces because `-mod=vendor` conflicts with workspace mode; `go-overlay`'s `buildGoWorkspace` works around this limitation.

`go-overlay` avoids the circular dependency issue by having `govendor` and `buildGoApplication` communicate only via the manifest file.

### Workflow for Getting the Hash
You can start with `fakeHash` and then update it with the hash printed in the build failure message. This remains the standard approach in nixpkgs for `buildGoModule`.

---

The search didn't return specific results about **nvfetcher** being used to automatically update `vendorHash` for Go modules. nvfetcher is generally a source-update tool for Nix and may not natively handle vendorHash recalculation. If you're looking for a fully automated pipeline, the `go-overlay` / `govendor` approach or `gomod2nix` (which eliminates the need for a `vendorHash` entirely) may be more suitable solutions. Would you like me to search more specifically for nvfetcher and Go vendorHash automation?

## Go の vendorHash 自動更新

**nvfetcher には Go 向けの `cargo_lock` 相当機能はありません。** Rust 専用です。

### 選択肢

**1. misumisumi 方式のスクリプト（現実的）**

`update-mvn-hash.sh` と同じアプローチで、`nix build` を実行 → hash mismatch のエラーから正しいハッシュを抽出 → toml/json/nix を sed で置換するスクリプトを書く。

```bash
# イメージ
nix build .#aqua 2>&1 | grep 'got:' | cut -d: -f2-
# → 正しい vendorHash が得られる
```

**2. [gomod2nix](https://deepwiki.com/nix-community/gomod2nix)**

`buildGoModule` の代わりに `buildGoApplication` を使い、`gomod2nix.toml` で依存関係を管理する。`vendorHash` 自体が不要になる。ただし `buildGoModule` とはビルド方式が異なり、gomod2nix CLI でロックファイルを生成する必要がある。

**3. [go-overlay](https://github.com/purpleclay/go-overlay)**

`vendorHash` を不要にする新しいアプローチ。Go workspace もサポート。ただし flake input として追加が必要で、nixpkgs 標準の `buildGoModule` からの移行になる。

### 推奨

対象が aqua と entire の2パッケージだけなので、**方式1（ビルドエラーからハッシュ抽出するスクリプト）** が最もシンプルです。gomod2nix や go-overlay は仕組み自体を変えることになり、2パッケージのためにはオーバーキルです。

ワークフローにスクリプトを組み込みますか？

Here's a summary of the information found about **nixpkgs `passthru.updateScript`**, **`nix-update-script`**, and automatic package updates (including `vendorHash`):

## `passthru.updateScript` in Nixpkgs

Update scripts can be added to packages by setting `passthru.updateScript`. This is then executed by r-ryantm to create an update pull request. It can be manually invoked via `nix-shell maintainers/scripts/update.nix --argstr package <packagename>`.

## `nix-update-script`

The simplest way to enable automatic updates is:

```nix
passthru.updateScript = nix-update-script { };
```

If you want functionality similar to that of `unstableGitUpdater` but with the additional handlers offered by `nix-update`, use: `passthru.updateScript = nix-update-script { extraArgs = [ "--version=branch" ]; };`

## `nix-update` and `vendorHash` Support

`nix-update` can automatically figure out the latest version of packages from BitBucket, Codeberg, crates.io, Gitea, GitHub, GitLab, PyPi, RubyGems.org, Sourcehut, Savannah, and can update `buildGoModule`'s `vendorHash`/`vendorSha256`, `buildRustPackage`'s `cargoHash`/`cargoSha256`/`cargoLock`, `buildNpmPackage`'s `npmDepsHash`, `buildComposerProject`'s `vendorHash`, `buildMavenPackage`'s `mvnHash`, and more.

This is a key advantage of `nix-update-script` over simpler updaters like `gitUpdater`:

The `gitUpdater` only regenerates the source hash and is therefore unsuitable for package definitions with more than one FOD hash (e.g. Rust's `cargoHash`).

## `nix-update` vs `nixpkgs-update`

`nixpkgs-update` is optimized for mass-updates in nixpkgs while `nix-update` is better suited for interactive usage that might require user-intervention, i.e. fixing the build and testing the result.

`nixpkgs-update` is the code used by the GitHub bot @r-ryantm to automatically update nixpkgs. It uses package repository information from Repology.org, the GitHub releases API, and the package `passthru.updateScript` to generate lists of outdated packages.

## Standardization Efforts

Automatic package update scripts are rather fragmented. There are several ways of updating packages: `passthru.updateScript` is the most universal at the moment; `nix-update` is used by @r-ryantm, and also calls `passthru.updateScript`.

There's ongoing discussion about whether adding `nix-update-script { }` stubs everywhere is the right approach, with proposals that `stdenv.mkDerivation`'s `src` attribute could specify how to auto-update, so that sources using `fetchFromGitHub` and their siblings would have a reasonable default update without requiring `nix-update` to handle everything.

## Practical Example

The commit for **open-stage-control** shows a real-world migration from a custom 51-line update shell script to a simple one-liner: `passthru.updateScript = nix-update-script { };` — replacing all the manual hash-updating logic, because `nix-update` handles `npmDepsHash` (and `vendorHash`) automatically.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

> AGENT

良い質問です。これは根本的に異なるアプローチです。

## `passthru.updateScript` / `nix-update` とは

[nix-update](https://github.com/Mic92/nix-update) は `.nix` ファイルを**直接書き換える**ツールで、以下を全て自動で更新できます：

- バージョン（GitHub tags, PyPI, crates.io 等から検出）
- ソースハッシュ
- **`vendorHash`**（Go）
- **`cargoHash`** / `cargoLock`（Rust）
- **`npmDepsHash`**（npm）
- **`pnpmDepsHash`**（pnpm）

つまり nvfetcher が苦手とする依存ハッシュも全て対応しています。

## nvfetcher vs nix-update

| | nvfetcher | nix-update |
|---|---|---|
| ソースハッシュ | 自動 | 自動 |
| vendorHash (Go) | 手動 / スクリプト | **自動** |
| cargoHash (Rust) | `cargo_lock` で回避 | **自動** |
| npmDepsHash | 手動 / スクリプト | **自動** |
| pnpmDepsHash | 手動 / スクリプト | **自動** |
| 仕組み | toml + generated.nix（間接参照） | .nix ファイルを直接 sed 的に書き換え |
| オーバーレイ構造 | リファクタ必要 | **そのまま使える** |

## 結論

**元の `final: prev:` 形式のオーバーレイに対して `nix-update` を CI で回すだけで、nvfetcher なしに全部丸まります。** nvfetcher への移行自体が不要だった可能性があります。

どうしますか？nvfetcher を捨てて `nix-update` ベースに切り替えることもできます。差分は元のオーバーレイ形式に戻す + CI ワークフローで `nix-update` を回す形になります。

- [Discussion about auto-updating packages - nixpkgs#467806](https://github.com/nixos/nixpkgs/issues/467806)

Here's a summary of what I found comparing **nvfetcher** and **nix-update** for Nix package updates:

---

## nvfetcher vs nix-update: Overview & Comparison

### **nvfetcher** (by berberman)

nvfetcher is a tool to automate nix package updates, built on top of Shake and integrating nvchecker.

**Key approach:** Unlike some other tools, nvfetcher is **non-intrusive** — it generates a nix source expr file that can be "injected" into package derivations, rather than rewriting something.

- You tell nvfetcher how to get the latest version number of packages and how to fetch their sources given version numbers, and it keeps their version and prefetched SHA256 sums up-to-date, producing ready-to-use nix expressions in `_sources/generated.nix`.
- The nvfetcher CLI program accepts a TOML file as config, which defines a set of package sources to run.
- It's flexible — "how to track the version update of a package" and "how to fetch a package" are not correlated, so you can make arbitrary combinations of package source and fetcher.
- The nvfetcher package is available in nixpkgs, and can also be used as a Haskell library on Hackage.

**Popularity:** nvfetcher has approximately **210 GitHub stars**, 3 watchers, 17 forks, and 22 open issues. Written in **Haskell**.

---

### **nix-update** (by Mic92)

nix-update updates versions/source hashes of nix packages. It is designed to work with nixpkgs but also other package sets.

**Key approach:** nix-update directly patches existing Nix files in-place with updated versions and hashes, designed for interactive use.

- It can automatically figure out the latest version of packages from various sources and generate lockfiles like `Cargo.lock`, `package-lock.json`, and build/run the resulting package.
- nix-update can optionally generate a commit message in the form `attribute: old_version -> new_version`.
- With the `--shell`, `--build`, `--test` and `--run` flags the update can be tested. Additionally, the `--review` flag can be used to initiate a run of nixpkgs-review.
- The latest release is **1.10.0**, which added support for updating dotnet packages, a `--option` argument, and a `--src-only` flag.

**Popularity:** nix-update has approximately **583 GitHub stars** and 63 forks — roughly **~2.8× more popular** than nvfetcher by stars. Written in **Python**. The project is actively maintained with recent issues from early 2025.

---

### Key Differences

| Feature | **nvfetcher** | **nix-update** |
|---|---|---|
| **Approach** | Generates a separate `_sources/generated.nix` (non-intrusive) | Patches existing Nix files in-place |
| **Config** | TOML config file (`nvfetcher.toml`) | CLI arguments per-package |
| **Best for** | Custom package repos, flakes overlays, batch tracking | nixpkgs contributions, interactive single-package updates |
| **Language** | Haskell | Python |
| **GitHub Stars** | ~210 | ~583 |
| **Rust support** | Supports extracting `Cargo.lock` and calculating `cargoLock.outputHashes` automatically. | Can generate `Cargo.lock` and `package-lock.json` lockfiles. |
| **Build/test integration** | No built-in build/test | Supports `--build`, `--test`, `--shell`, `--run`, and `--review` flags. |

### Also notable:
- **nixpkgs-update** (a separate tool) is optimized for mass-updates in nixpkgs, while nix-update is better suited for interactive usage that might require user intervention.
- nvfetcher can track updates to a specific branch and automatically update your nix sources configuration on each run to the tip of that branch.

### Bottom Line

**nix-update** is more popular and widely used, especially for contributors to nixpkgs who need to interactively update individual packages with testing. **nvfetcher** is preferred for users maintaining their own package sets or flake-based repos where a declarative TOML-based approach to tracking many upstream sources is more convenient. No direct 2025/2026-specific blog posts or articles comparing the two tools head-to-head were found in the search results, suggesting these tools serve somewhat different niches in the Nix ecosystem.

---

## Research Findings: nix-update with Custom Overlays in a Flake

### 1. Core Problem: nix-update and Overlay-Only Packages

**nix-update (`Mic92/nix-update`) does work with flakes**, but there is an important structural requirement: your packages must be exposed as flake output attributes. When you use `--flake`, nix-update resolves packages by looking at:

1. `packages.${currentSystem}.${attribute}` (first attempt)
2. `flake.${attribute}` (fallback)

Your current `flake.nix` at `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/flake.nix` does **not** expose the overlay packages (aqua, polycat, keifu, similarity-ts, pretty-ts-errors-markdown, etc.) via a `packages` flake output. They are only applied internally via `nixpkgs.overlays` inside `mkSystem` and `mkDarwin`. This means **`nix-update --flake` cannot currently find them**.

### 2. Supported Hash Types

nix-update supports updating all of the hash types used in your overlays:

| Hash Type | Builder | Your Overlays Using It |
|---|---|---|
| `hash` (src) | `fetchFromGitHub` | All overlays |
| `vendorHash` | `buildGoModule` | `aqua.nix`, `difit.nix`, `jj-desc.nix` |
| `cargoHash` | `buildRustPackage` | `similarity-ts.nix`, `keifu.nix` |
| `npmDepsHash` | `buildNpmPackage` | `pretty-ts-errors-markdown.nix` |
| `pnpmDepsHash` | `buildNpmPackage` (pnpm) | (none currently, but supported) |

The complete list of hash updaters in nix-update's codebase:

- `go_modules` / `go_modules_old` -- `vendorHash` / `vendorSha256`
- `cargo_deps` / `cargo_vendor_deps` -- `cargoHash` / `cargoSha256` / `cargoLock`
- `npm_deps` -- `npmDepsHash`
- `pnpm_deps` -- `pnpmDepsHash`
- `composer_deps` / `composer_deps_old` -- composer `vendorHash`
- `maven_deps` -- `mvnHash`
- `mix_deps` -- `mixFodDeps`
- `yarn_deps` / `yarn_deps_old` -- `yarnOfflineCache` / `offlineCache`
- `zig_deps` -- `zigDeps`
- `nugetDeps` (handled separately)
- `cargo_lock` -- lockfile generation

### 3. How to Make It Work: Expose Packages in Flake Output

To use `nix-update --flake`, you need to add a `packages` output to your flake. Since you use `flake-parts`, this would go in the `perSystem` section:

```nix
perSystem = { pkgs, system, config, ... }:
let
  customPkgs = import nixpkgs {
    inherit system;
    overlays = [ (import ./nix/overlays) ];
  };
in {
  packages = {
    aqua = customPkgs.aqua;
    polycat = customPkgs.polycat;
    keifu = customPkgs.keifu;
    similarity-ts = customPkgs.similarity-ts;
    pretty-ts-errors-markdown = customPkgs.pretty-ts-errors-markdown;
    difit = customPkgs.difit;
    jj-desc = customPkgs.jj-desc;
    entire = customPkgs.entire;
  };
  # ... rest of perSystem
};
```

### 4. CLI Commands (After Exposing Packages)

**Update to latest GitHub release:**
```bash
# Go packages (updates src hash + vendorHash)
nix-update aqua --flake

# Rust packages (updates src hash + cargoHash)
nix-update similarity-ts --flake
nix-update keifu --flake

# npm packages (updates src hash + npmDepsHash)
nix-update pretty-ts-errors-markdown --flake

# C/C++ packages (updates src hash only)
nix-update polycat --flake
```

**Update to a specific version:**
```bash
nix-update aqua --flake --version=2.56.0
```

**Update unstable/branch-tracking packages (like polycat which tracks `main`):**
```bash
nix-update polycat --flake --version=branch
# or for a specific branch:
nix-update polycat --flake --version=branch=main
```

**Skip version bump, only refresh hashes:**
```bash
nix-update aqua --flake --version=skip
```

**Build after updating to verify:**
```bash
nix-update aqua --flake --build
```

**Auto-commit the change:**
```bash
nix-update aqua --flake --build --commit
# Generates commit message: "aqua: 2.55.3 -> 2.56.0"
```

**Override the file location (if auto-detection fails):**
```bash
nix-update aqua --flake --override-filename nix/overlays/aqua.nix
```

**Update subpackages (for packages with multiple FODs):**
```bash
nix-update some-package --flake --subpackage web-ui --subpackage api
```

**Specify target system:**
```bash
nix-update aqua --flake --system x86_64-linux
```

### 5. passthru.updateScript / nix-update-script Outside nixpkgs

There are two distinct mechanisms:

**A. `nix-update-script` (the nixpkgs helper)**

This is defined in nixpkgs at `pkgs/common-updaters/nix-update-script.nix`. It is designed to be used **inside nixpkgs** as:
```nix
passthru.updateScript = nix-update-script { };
```
It is then invoked by `maintainers/scripts/update.nix` or by r-ryantm. This does **not** directly work outside nixpkgs without custom wrapper scripts.

**B. `nix-update --use-update-script` (the CLI flag)**

When you run `nix-update` with `--use-update-script`, it checks whether the package has a `passthru.updateScript` and executes it. For flake packages, it builds the updateScript and executes it within a `nix develop` shell. This **does** work outside nixpkgs:

```bash
nix-update aqua --flake --use-update-script
```

However, your overlay packages currently do not define `passthru.updateScript`, so this flag would have no effect on them. If you wanted to add it, you could do:

```nix
# In aqua.nix overlay
final: prev: {
  aqua = final.buildGoModule rec {
    pname = "aqua";
    version = "2.55.3";
    # ... existing attrs ...

    passthru.updateScript = final.nix-update-script { };
  };
}
```

Then update via:
```bash
nix-update aqua --flake --use-update-script
```

**C. Running nixpkgs `maintainers/scripts/update.nix` with overlays**

The nixpkgs `maintainers/scripts/update.nix` has an `include-overlays` parameter. You can theoretically pass your overlay to it:
```bash
nix-shell maintainers/scripts/update.nix \
  --argstr package aqua \
  --arg include-overlays '[ (import /path/to/your/overlay.nix) ]'
```
This approach is fragile and not recommended compared to using `nix-update --flake` directly.

### 6. Practical Recommendation for This Repository

The simplest path forward is:

1. Add a `packages` output to the flake (as shown in section 3 above)
2. Run nix-update directly, for example:
   ```bash
   cd /home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix
   nix-shell -p nix-update
   nix-update aqua --flake --build --commit
   ```
3. Or install nix-update via your flake and create a simple update script:
   ```bash
   #!/usr/bin/env bash
   for pkg in aqua polycat keifu similarity-ts pretty-ts-errors-markdown difit jj-desc entire; do
     echo "Updating $pkg..."
     nix-update "$pkg" --flake --build --commit || true
   done
   ```

### Key Limitations

- **nix-update cannot update packages that are not exposed as flake outputs** -- overlay-only packages are invisible to `--flake`
- **File auto-detection can fail** for non-standard directory structures. Your overlays live at `nix/overlays/<name>.nix`, which is non-standard. You may need `--override-filename` if auto-detection picks the wrong file
- **`nix-update-script`** (the nixpkgs passthru helper) is designed for nixpkgs automation (r-ryantm). Outside nixpkgs, use `nix-update` CLI directly instead
- **polycat** tracks `rev = "main"` without a versioned tag, so you would need `--version=branch` for it

### Sources

- [Mic92/nix-update GitHub repository](https://github.com/Mic92/nix-update)
- [nix-update README](https://github.com/Mic92/nix-update/blob/main/README.md)
- [Nixpkgs Update Scripts (Official NixOS Wiki)](https://wiki.nixos.org/wiki/Nixpkgs/Update_Scripts)
- [How can I run the updateScript of personal packages? (NixOS Discourse)](https://discourse.nixos.org/t/how-can-i-run-the-updatescript-of-personal-packages/25274)
- [Best approach for updateScript when there are multiple hashes (NixOS Discourse)](https://discourse.nixos.org/t/best-approach-for-updatescript-when-there-are-multiple-hashes/42897)
- [Mastering Nixpkgs Overlays (Nixcademy)](https://nixcademy.com/posts/mastering-nixpkgs-overlays-techniques-and-best-practice/)
- [Mic92/nix-update on FlakeHub](https://flakehub.com/flake/Mic92/nix-update)

   315→                  }
   316→                  echo "Done!"

   319→            };

   321→              type = "app";

   335→              shfmt.enable = true;
   336→            };
   337→            settings.global.excludes = [

   341→          };

   356→              };
   357→            };
   358→          };
   359→

   363→            '';
   364→          };
   365→        };

    10→        description: "Minimum release age in days before updating"

     1→# Thanks: https://github.com/ryoppippi/dotfiles/blob/main/.github/workflows/_update-flake-reusable.yaml
     2→name: Update Flake Inputs (Reusable)
     3→
     4→on:
     5→  workflow_call:
     6→    inputs:
     7→      inputs:
     8→        description: Space-separated list of specific inputs to update (empty for all)
     9→        type: string
    10→        default: ""
    11→      exclude-inputs:
    12→        description: Space-separated list of inputs to exclude from discovery
    13→        type: string
    14→        default: ""
    15→      skip-delay-inputs:
    16→        description: Space-separated list of inputs that should skip the release age check
    17→        type: string
    18→        default: ""
    19→      minimum-release-age-days:
    20→        description: Minimum release age in days before updating
    21→        type: string
    22→        default: "3"
    23→      skip-delay:
    24→        description: Skip the minimum release age check for all inputs
    25→        type: boolean
    26→        default: false
    27→      pr-labels:
    28→        description: Labels to add to PRs (comma-separated)
    29→        type: string
    30→        default: "dependencies,automated"
    31→      auto-merge:
    32→        description: Enable auto-merge for PRs
    33→        type: boolean
    34→        default: true
    35→      summary-title:
    36→        description: Title for the summary section
    37→        type: string
    38→        default: "Update Summary"
    39→    secrets:
    40→      app-id:
    41→        required: true
    42→      private-key:
    43→        required: true
    44→      cachix-auth-token:
    45→        required: false
    46→
    47→jobs:
    48→  discover:
    49→    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm
    50→    outputs:
    51→      matrix: ${{ steps.discover.outputs.matrix }}
    52→      has-updates: ${{ steps.discover.outputs.has-updates }}
    53→    steps:
    54→      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2
    55→      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-nix
    56→      - id: discover
    57→        uses: ./.github/actions/discover-flake-inputs
    58→        with:
    59→          inputs: ${{ inputs.inputs }}
    60→          exclude-inputs: ${{ inputs.exclude-inputs }}
    61→          skip-delay-inputs: ${{ inputs.skip-delay-inputs }}
    62→
    63→  update:
    64→    needs: discover
    65→    if: needs.discover.outputs.has-updates == 'true'
    66→    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm
    67→    strategy:
    68→      fail-fast: false
    69→      matrix: ${{ fromJSON(needs.discover.outputs.matrix) }}
    70→    permissions:
    71→      contents: write
    72→      pull-requests: write
    73→    steps:
    74→      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2
    75→
    76→      - id: setup-bot
    77→        uses: ./.github/actions/setup-git-bot
    78→        with:
    79→          app-id: ${{ secrets.app-id }}
    80→          app-private-key: ${{ secrets.private-key }}
    81→
    82→      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2
    83→        with:
    84→          token: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}
    85→
    86→      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-nix
    87→        with:
    88→          cachix-auth-token: ${{ secrets.cachix-auth-token }}
    89→
    90→      - uses: ./.github/actions/update-flake-input
    91→        with:
    92→          input-name: ${{ matrix.name }}
    93→          current-version: ${{ matrix.current_version }}
    94→          owner: ${{ matrix.owner }}
    95→          repo: ${{ matrix.repo }}
    96→          ref: ${{ matrix.ref }}
    97→          skip-delay: ${{ inputs.skip-delay && 'true' || matrix.skip_delay }}
    98→          minimum-release-age-days: ${{ inputs.minimum-release-age-days }}
    99→          auto-merge: ${{ inputs.auto-merge && 'true' || 'false' }}
   100→          pr-labels: ${{ inputs.pr-labels }}
   101→          github-token: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}
   102→
   103→  summary:
   104→    needs: [discover, update]
   105→    if: always() && needs.discover.outputs.has-updates == 'true'
   106→    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm
   107→    steps:
   108→      - name: Generate summary
   109→        env:
   110→          SUMMARY_TITLE: ${{ inputs.summary-title }}
   111→          INPUTS: ${{ inputs.inputs }}
   112→          EXCLUDE_INPUTS: ${{ inputs.exclude-inputs }}
   113→          SKIP_DELAY_INPUTS: ${{ inputs.skip-delay-inputs }}
   114→          MIN_AGE_DAYS: ${{ inputs.minimum-release-age-days }}
   115→          AUTO_MERGE: ${{ inputs.auto-merge }}
   116→          UPDATE_RESULT: ${{ needs.update.result }}
   117→        run: |
   118→          echo "## $SUMMARY_TITLE" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   119→          echo "" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   120→          echo "### Configuration" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   121→          if [ -n "$INPUTS" ]; then
   122→            echo "- Inputs: $INPUTS" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   123→          fi
   124→          if [ -n "$EXCLUDE_INPUTS" ]; then
   125→            echo "- Excluded: $EXCLUDE_INPUTS" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   126→          fi
   127→          if [ -n "$SKIP_DELAY_INPUTS" ]; then
   128→            echo "- Skip delay: $SKIP_DELAY_INPUTS" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   129→          fi
   130→          echo "- Minimum release age: $MIN_AGE_DAYS days" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   131→          echo "- Auto-merge: $AUTO_MERGE" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   132→          echo "" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   133→
   134→          if [ "$UPDATE_RESULT" = "failure" ]; then
   135→            echo ":warning: Some updates failed. Check individual job logs for details." >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   136→          else
   137→            echo ":white_check_mark: All update jobs completed." >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
   138→          fi
   139→
```

     1→# Thanks: https://github.com/ryoppippi/dotfiles/blob/main/.github/actions/setup-git-bot/action.yaml
     2→name: Setup Git Bot
     3→description: Generate GitHub App token and configure git identity
     4→
     5→inputs:
     6→  app-id:
     7→    description: GitHub App ID
     8→    required: true
     9→  app-private-key:
    10→    description: GitHub App private key
    11→    required: true
    12→
    13→outputs:
    14→  token:
    15→    description: GitHub App installation token
    16→    value: ${{ steps.token.outputs.token }}
    17→
    18→runs:
    19→  using: composite
    20→  steps:
    21→    - id: token
    22→      uses: actions/create-github-app-token@bf559f85448f9380bcfa2899dbdc01eb5b37be3a # v3.0.0-beta.2
    23→      with:
    24→        app-id: ${{ inputs.app-id }}
    25→        private-key: ${{ inputs.app-private-key }}
    26→
    27→    - shell: bash
    28→      env:
    29→        GH_TOKEN: ${{ steps.token.outputs.token }}
    30→        APP_SLUG: ${{ steps.token.outputs.app-slug }}
    31→      run: |
    32→        BOT_ID=$(gh api "users/${APP_SLUG}[bot]" --jq '.id')
    33→        git config user.name "${APP_SLUG}[bot]"
    34→        git config user.email "${BOT_ID}+${APP_SLUG}[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
    35→

     1→# Thanks: https://github.com/ryoppippi/dotfiles/blob/main/.github/actions/update-flake-input/action.yaml
     2→name: Update Flake Input
     3→description: Update a single Nix flake input with optional release age check and auto-merge
     4→
     5→inputs:
     6→  input-name:
     7→    description: Name of the flake input to update
     8→    required: true
     9→  current-version:
    10→    description: Current version/revision of the input
    11→    required: true
    12→  owner:
    13→    description: GitHub owner of the input repository
    14→    required: false
    15→    default: ""
    16→  repo:
    17→    description: GitHub repository name of the input
    18→    required: false
    19→    default: ""
    20→  ref:
    21→    description: Git ref (branch/tag) of the input
    22→    required: false
    23→    default: ""
    24→  skip-delay:
    25→    description: Skip the minimum release age check
    26→    required: false
    27→    default: "false"
    28→  minimum-release-age-days:
    29→    description: Minimum release age in days before updating
    30→    required: false
    31→    default: "3"
    32→  auto-merge:
    33→    description: Enable auto-merge for the PR
    34→    required: false
    35→    default: "true"
    36→  pr-labels:
    37→    description: Comma-separated labels to add to the PR
    38→    required: false
    39→    default: "dependencies,automated"
    40→  github-token:
    41→    description: GitHub token for creating PRs and enabling auto-merge
    42→    required: true
    43→
    44→outputs:
    45→  updated:
    46→    description: Whether the input was updated
    47→    value: ${{ steps.update.outputs.updated }}
    48→  new-version:
    49→    description: New version/revision after update
    50→    value: ${{ steps.update.outputs.new_version }}
    51→  pr-url:
    52→    description: URL of the created/updated PR
    53→    value: ${{ steps.create-pr.outputs.pr_url }}
    54→
    55→runs:
    56→  using: composite
    57→  steps:
    58→    - name: Update flake input
    59→      id: update
    60→      shell: bash
    61→      env:
    62→        INPUT_NAME: ${{ inputs.input-name }}
    63→        INPUT_OWNER: ${{ inputs.owner }}
    64→        INPUT_REPO: ${{ inputs.repo }}
    65→        INPUT_REF: ${{ inputs.ref }}
    66→        SKIP_DELAY: ${{ inputs.skip-delay }}
    67→        MIN_AGE_DAYS: ${{ inputs.minimum-release-age-days }}
    68→        GH_TOKEN: ${{ inputs.github-token }}
    69→      run: |
    70→        set -euo pipefail
    71→
    72→        echo "=== Updating input: $INPUT_NAME ==="
    73→        echo "Skip delay: $SKIP_DELAY"
    74→        echo "Minimum release age: $MIN_AGE_DAYS days"
    75→
    76→        min_age_seconds=$((MIN_AGE_DAYS * 86400))
    77→
    78→        # Get current revision before update
    79→        current_metadata=$(nix flake metadata --json --no-write-lock-file 2>/dev/null)
    80→        current_rev=$(echo "$current_metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$INPUT_NAME\".locked.rev // \"unknown\"")
    81→        current_owner=$(echo "$current_metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$INPUT_NAME\".locked.owner // \"unknown\"")
    82→        current_repo=$(echo "$current_metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$INPUT_NAME\".locked.repo // \"unknown\"")
    83→        echo "Current revision: $current_rev"
    84→
    85→        # Function to get package versions from a flake
    86→        get_package_versions() {
    87→          local flake_ref="$1"
    88→          local packages=""
    89→
    90→          case "$INPUT_NAME" in
    91→            llm-agents)
    92→              packages="claude-code ccusage codex opencode vibe-kanban"
    93→              ;;
    94→            *)
    95→              echo ""
    96→              return
    97→              ;;
    98→          esac
    99→
   100→          local versions=""
   101→          for pkg in $packages; do
   102→            local version
   103→            version=$(nix eval --raw "${flake_ref}#packages.x86_64-linux.${pkg}.version" 2>/dev/null || echo "")
   104→            if [ -n "$version" ]; then
   105→              if [ -n "$versions" ]; then
   106→                versions="${versions}, ${pkg}=${version}"
   107→              else
   108→                versions="${pkg}=${version}"
   109→              fi
   110→            fi
   111→          done
   112→          echo "$versions"
   113→        }
   114→
   115→        # Get current package versions for special inputs
   116→        current_pkg_versions=""
   117→        if [ "$INPUT_NAME" = "llm-agents" ]; then
   118→          echo "Fetching current package versions..."
   119→          current_pkg_versions=$(get_package_versions "github:${current_owner}/${current_repo}/${current_rev}")
   120→          echo "Current package versions: $current_pkg_versions"
   121→        fi
   122→
   123→        # Determine target revision for update
   124→        target_rev=""
   125→        if [ "$SKIP_DELAY" = "false" ] && [ -n "$INPUT_OWNER" ] && [ -n "$INPUT_REPO" ]; then
   126→          echo "Fetching commit from at least $MIN_AGE_DAYS days ago via GitHub API..."
   127→
   128→          cutoff_date=$(date -u -d "$MIN_AGE_DAYS days ago" +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ 2>/dev/null || \
   129→                        date -u -v-${MIN_AGE_DAYS}d +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)
   130→          echo "Cutoff date: $cutoff_date"
   131→
   132→          api_url="repos/${INPUT_OWNER}/${INPUT_REPO}/commits?until=${cutoff_date}&per_page=1"
   133→          if [ -n "$INPUT_REF" ]; then
   134→            api_url="${api_url}&sha=${INPUT_REF}"
   135→            echo "Filtering by branch: $INPUT_REF"
   136→          fi
   137→
   138→          if commit_data=$(gh api "$api_url" --jq '.[0] | {sha: .sha, date: .commit.committer.date}' 2>/dev/null); then
   139→            target_rev=$(echo "$commit_data" | jq -r '.sha // empty')
   140→            commit_date=$(echo "$commit_data" | jq -r '.date // empty')
   141→
   142→            if [ -n "$target_rev" ]; then
   143→              echo "Found commit from $commit_date: ${target_rev:0:8}"
   144→
   145→              if [ "${target_rev:0:40}" = "${current_rev:0:40}" ]; then
   146→                echo "Target revision is the same as current. No update needed."
   147→                echo "updated=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   148→                exit 0
   149→              fi
   150→
   151→              # Check if target is older than current (would be a downgrade)
   152→              if [ -n "$current_rev" ] && [ "$current_rev" != "unknown" ]; then
   153→                current_commit_date=$(gh api "repos/${INPUT_OWNER}/${INPUT_REPO}/commits/${current_rev}" --jq '.commit.committer.date' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
   154→                if [ -n "$current_commit_date" ] && [ -n "$commit_date" ]; then
   155→                  current_ts=$(date -d "$current_commit_date" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
   156→                  target_ts=$(date -d "$commit_date" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
   157→                  if [ "$target_ts" -lt "$current_ts" ]; then
   158→                    echo "Target revision (${target_rev:0:8} from $commit_date) is older than current. Skipping to avoid downgrade."
   159→                    echo "updated=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   160→                    exit 0
   161→                  fi
   162→                fi
   163→              fi
   164→            else
   165→              echo "No commits found older than $MIN_AGE_DAYS days. Skipping update."
   166→              echo "updated=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   167→              exit 0
   168→            fi
   169→          else
   170→            echo "Warning: Failed to fetch commits via GitHub API, falling back to standard update"
   171→          fi
   172→        fi
   173→
   174→        # Perform the update
   175→        if [ -n "$target_rev" ]; then
   176→          echo "Running: nix flake update $INPUT_NAME --override-input $INPUT_NAME github:${INPUT_OWNER}/${INPUT_REPO}/${target_rev}"
   177→          if ! nix flake update "$INPUT_NAME" --override-input "$INPUT_NAME" "github:${INPUT_OWNER}/${INPUT_REPO}/${target_rev}"; then
   178→            echo "::error::Failed to update $INPUT_NAME to specific revision"
   179→            exit 1
   180→          fi
   181→        else
   182→          echo "Running: nix flake update $INPUT_NAME"
   183→          if ! nix flake update "$INPUT_NAME"; then
   184→            echo "::error::Failed to update $INPUT_NAME"
   185→            exit 1
   186→          fi
   187→        fi
   188→
   189→        # Check if there were actual changes
   190→        if git diff --quiet flake.lock; then
   191→          echo "No changes detected for $INPUT_NAME"
   192→          echo "updated=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   193→          exit 0
   194→        fi
   195→
   196→        # Get new revision info
   197→        new_metadata=$(nix flake metadata --json --no-write-lock-file 2>/dev/null)
   198→        new_rev=$(echo "$new_metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$INPUT_NAME\".locked.rev // \"unknown\"")
   199→        new_owner=$(echo "$new_metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$INPUT_NAME\".locked.owner // \"unknown\"")
   200→        new_repo=$(echo "$new_metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$INPUT_NAME\".locked.repo // \"unknown\"")
   201→        echo "New revision: $new_rev"
   202→
   203→        # Get new package versions for special inputs
   204→        new_pkg_versions=""
   205→        pkg_version_diff=""
   206→        if [ "$INPUT_NAME" = "llm-agents" ]; then
   207→          echo "Fetching new package versions..."
   208→          new_pkg_versions=$(get_package_versions "github:${new_owner}/${new_repo}/${new_rev}")
   209→          echo "New package versions: $new_pkg_versions"
   210→
   211→          if [ -n "$current_pkg_versions" ] && [ -n "$new_pkg_versions" ]; then
   212→            declare -A old_versions new_versions
   213→            IFS=', ' read -ra old_pairs <<< "$current_pkg_versions"
   214→            for pair in "${old_pairs[@]}"; do
   215→              key="${pair%%=*}"
   216→              val="${pair#*=}"
   217→              old_versions["$key"]="$val"
   218→            done
   219→            IFS=', ' read -ra new_pairs <<< "$new_pkg_versions"
   220→            for pair in "${new_pairs[@]}"; do
   221→              key="${pair%%=*}"
   222→              val="${pair#*=}"
   223→              new_versions["$key"]="$val"
   224→            done
   225→
   226→            changed_pkgs=""
   227→            for key in "${!new_versions[@]}"; do
   228→              old_val="${old_versions[$key]:-}"
   229→              new_val="${new_versions[$key]}"
   230→              if [ "$old_val" != "$new_val" ] && [ -n "$old_val" ]; then
   231→                if [ -n "$changed_pkgs" ]; then
   232→                  changed_pkgs="${changed_pkgs}, ${key} ${old_val} → ${new_val}"
   233→                else
   234→                  changed_pkgs="${key} ${old_val} → ${new_val}"
   235→                fi
   236→              fi
   237→            done
   238→
   239→            if [ -n "$changed_pkgs" ]; then
   240→              pkg_version_diff="$changed_pkgs"
   241→              echo "Package version changes: $pkg_version_diff"
   242→            fi
   243→          fi
   244→        fi
   245→
   246→        # Verify release age for standard updates
   247→        if [ -z "$target_rev" ] && [ "$SKIP_DELAY" = "false" ]; then
   248→          echo "Verifying release age (minimum: $MIN_AGE_DAYS days)..."
   249→          last_modified=$(echo "$new_metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$INPUT_NAME\".locked.lastModified // 0")
   250→
   251→          if [ "$last_modified" != "0" ] && [ "$last_modified" != "null" ]; then
   252→            current_time=$(date +%s)
   253→            age_seconds=$((current_time - last_modified))
   254→            age_days=$((age_seconds / 86400))
   255→            echo "Release age: $age_days days"
   256→
   257→            if [ "$age_seconds" -lt "$min_age_seconds" ]; then
   258→              echo "Release is too new (less than $MIN_AGE_DAYS days old). Skipping update."
   259→              git checkout flake.lock
   260→              echo "updated=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   261→              exit 0
   262→            fi
   263→            echo "Release age check passed (>= $MIN_AGE_DAYS days old)"
   264→          fi
   265→        fi
   266→
   267→        echo "Update successful!"
   268→        echo "updated=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   269→        echo "new_version=${new_rev:0:8}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   270→        echo "current_pkg_versions=${current_pkg_versions}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   271→        echo "new_pkg_versions=${new_pkg_versions}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   272→        echo "pkg_version_diff=${pkg_version_diff}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   273→
   274→    - name: Create pull request
   275→      id: create-pr
   276→      if: steps.update.outputs.updated == 'true'
   277→      shell: bash
   278→      env:
   279→        GH_TOKEN: ${{ inputs.github-token }}
   280→        INPUT_NAME: ${{ inputs.input-name }}
   281→        CURRENT_VERSION: ${{ inputs.current-version }}
   282→        NEW_VERSION: ${{ steps.update.outputs.new_version }}
   283→        AUTO_MERGE: ${{ inputs.auto-merge }}
   284→        PR_LABELS: ${{ inputs.pr-labels }}
   285→        SKIP_DELAY: ${{ inputs.skip-delay }}
   286→        MIN_AGE_DAYS: ${{ inputs.minimum-release-age-days }}
   287→        CURRENT_PKG_VERSIONS: ${{ steps.update.outputs.current_pkg_versions }}
   288→        NEW_PKG_VERSIONS: ${{ steps.update.outputs.new_pkg_versions }}
   289→        PKG_VERSION_DIFF: ${{ steps.update.outputs.pkg_version_diff }}
   290→      run: |
   291→        set -euo pipefail
   292→
   293→        branch="update-flake-${INPUT_NAME}"
   294→
   295→        # Build PR title
   296→        if [ -n "$PKG_VERSION_DIFF" ]; then
   297→          pr_title="flake.lock: update ${INPUT_NAME} (${PKG_VERSION_DIFF})"
   298→        else
   299→          pr_title="flake.lock: update ${INPUT_NAME} to ${NEW_VERSION}"
   300→        fi
   301→
   302→        # Build delay note
   303→        if [ "$SKIP_DELAY" = "true" ]; then
   304→          delay_note=":zap: This input skips the release age check."
   305→        else
   306→          delay_note=":hourglass: Passed the ${MIN_AGE_DAYS}-day minimum release age check."
   307→        fi
   308→
   309→        # Build package versions table
   310→        pkg_versions_section=""
   311→        if [ -n "$NEW_PKG_VERSIONS" ]; then
   312→          pkg_versions_section="
   313→        ## Package Versions
   314→        | Package | Old | New |
   315→        |---------|-----|-----|"
   316→
   317→          declare -A old_versions new_versions
   318→          if [ -n "$CURRENT_PKG_VERSIONS" ]; then
   319→            IFS=', ' read -ra old_pairs <<< "$CURRENT_PKG_VERSIONS"
   320→            for pair in "${old_pairs[@]}"; do
   321→              key="${pair%%=*}"
   322→              val="${pair#*=}"
   323→              old_versions["$key"]="$val"
   324→            done
   325→          fi
   326→          IFS=', ' read -ra new_pairs <<< "$NEW_PKG_VERSIONS"
   327→          for pair in "${new_pairs[@]}"; do
   328→            key="${pair%%=*}"
   329→            val="${pair#*=}"
   330→            new_versions["$key"]="$val"
   331→          done
   332→
   333→          for key in "${!new_versions[@]}"; do
   334→            old_val="${old_versions[$key]:-N/A}"
   335→            new_val="${new_versions[$key]}"
   336→            if [ "$old_val" != "$new_val" ]; then
   337→              pkg_versions_section="${pkg_versions_section}
   338→        | ${key} | \`${old_val}\` | \`${new_val}\` :arrow_up: |"
   339→            else
   340→              pkg_versions_section="${pkg_versions_section}
   341→        | ${key} | \`${old_val}\` | \`${new_val}\` |"
   342→            fi
   343→          done
   344→        fi
   345→
   346→        pr_body="Automated update of flake input \`${INPUT_NAME}\`.
   347→
   348→        ## Changes
   349→        - **${INPUT_NAME}**: \`${CURRENT_VERSION}\` :arrow_right: \`${NEW_VERSION}\`
   350→        ${pkg_versions_section}
   351→
   352→        ## Notes
   353→        ${delay_note}
   354→
   355→        ---
   356→        This PR was automatically created by the flake update workflow."
   357→
   358→        # Stage, commit, push
   359→        git add flake.lock
   360→        git checkout -b "$branch"
   361→        git commit -m "$pr_title"
   362→        git push --force origin "$branch"
   363→
   364→        # Create or update PR
   365→        pr_number=$(gh pr list --head "$branch" --json number --jq '.[0].number // empty')
   366→
   367→        if [ -n "$pr_number" ]; then
   368→          echo "Updating existing PR #$pr_number"
   369→          gh pr edit "$pr_number" --title "$pr_title" --body "$pr_body"
   370→        else
   371→          echo "Creating new PR"
   372→          label_args=""
   373→          IFS=',' read -ra labels <<< "$PR_LABELS"
   374→          for label in "${labels[@]}"; do
   375→            label=$(echo "$label" | xargs)
   376→            label_args="$label_args --label $label"
   377→          done
   378→
   379→          gh pr create \
   380→            --title "$pr_title" \
   381→            --body "$pr_body" \
   382→            --base main \
   383→            --head "$branch" \
   384→            $label_args
   385→
   386→          pr_number=$(gh pr list --head "$branch" --json number --jq '.[0].number')
   387→        fi
   388→
   389→        # Enable auto-merge
   390→        if [ "$AUTO_MERGE" = "true" ] && [ -n "$pr_number" ]; then
   391→          echo "Enabling auto-merge for PR #$pr_number"
   392→          gh pr merge "$pr_number" --auto --squash || echo "Note: Auto-merge may require branch protection rules"
   393→        fi
   394→
   395→        pr_url=$(gh pr view "$pr_number" --json url --jq '.url')
   396→        echo "pr_url=$pr_url" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   397→        echo "PR URL: $pr_url"
   398→
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/.github/workflows/update-overlays.yaml",
  "content": "name: \"Update Custom Overlays\"\n\non:\n  schedule:\n    - cron: \"0 18 * * 5\" # Saturday 03:00 JST\n  workflow_dispatch:\n\npermissions:\n  contents: write\n  pull-requests: write\n\njobs:\n  update:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2\n        with:\n          persist-credentials: false\n\n      - id: setup-bot\n        uses: ./.github/actions/setup-git-bot\n        with:\n          app-id: ${{ secrets.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_ID }}\n          app-private-key: ${{ secrets.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}\n\n      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2\n        with:\n          token: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}\n\n      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-nix\n        with:\n          cachix-auth-token: ${{ secrets.CACHIX_AUTH_TOKEN }}\n\n      - name: Update overlay packages\n        run: |\n          nix shell nixpkgs#nix-update -c bash <<'SCRIPT'\n          set -uo pipefail\n\n          for pkg in aqua difit entire jj-desc keifu similarity-ts; do\n            echo \"::group::Updating $pkg\"\n            nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --commit || echo \"::warning::Failed to update $pkg\"\n            echo \"::endgroup::\"\n          done\n\n          for pkg in polycat pretty-ts-errors-markdown; do\n            echo \"::group::Updating $pkg\"\n            nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --version=branch --commit || echo \"::warning::Failed to update $pkg\"\n            echo \"::endgroup::\"\n          done\n          SCRIPT\n\n      - name: Format\n        run: nix fmt\n\n      - name: Commit formatting fixes\n        run: |\n          if ! git diff --quiet; then\n            git add -A\n            git commit -m \"style: format overlay files\"\n          fi\n\n      - name: Create pull request\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}\n        run: |\n          if [ \"$(git rev-list --count origin/main..HEAD)\" -eq 0 ]; then\n            echo \"No overlay updates found\"\n            exit 0\n          fi\n\n          branch=\"update-overlays\"\n          git push --force origin \"HEAD:refs/heads/$branch\"\n\n          commits=$(git log --oneline origin/main..HEAD | sed 's/^/- /')\n          body=\"Automated update of custom overlay packages via \\`nix-update\\`.\n\n          ## Commits\n\n          ${commits}\n\n          ---\n          This PR was automatically created by the overlay update workflow.\"\n\n          pr_number=$(gh pr list --head \"$branch\" --json number --jq '.[0].number // empty')\n\n          if [ -n \"$pr_number\" ]; then\n            gh pr edit \"$pr_number\" --title \"nix/overlays: update packages\" --body \"$body\"\n          else\n            gh pr create \\\n              --title \"nix/overlays: update packages\" \\\n              --body \"$body\" \\\n              --base main \\\n              --head \"$branch\" \\\n              --label dependencies --label automated\n\n            pr_number=$(gh pr list --head \"$branch\" --json number --jq '.[0].number')\n          fi\n\n          gh pr merge \"$pr_number\" --auto --squash || echo \"Note: Auto-merge may require branch protection rules\"\n"
}

nix-update が正しく動作しています:
1. `meta.position` から `nix/overlays/aqua.nix` を正しく検出
2. バージョン更新 `2.55.3 -> 2.56.6` を検出
3. src ハッシュを自動更新
4. vendorHash の更新は aqua v2.56.6 が Go 1.25.6 を要求するため失敗（nixpkgs の Go が 1.25.5 のため）

これは nix-update の問題ではなく nixpkgs の Go バージョンの問題です。ワークフローの `|| echo "::warning::"` で graceful に処理されます。

Rust パッケージでも試してみます。

> AGENT

変更内容のまとめ:
1. **`flake.nix`**: `perSystem` に `packages` 出力を追加（オーバーレイパッケージを `nix build .#<pkg>` で参照可能に）
2. **`.github/workflows/update-overlays.yaml`**: nix-update による週次自動更新ワークフロー

nvfetcher と比較した利点:
- オーバーレイファイル自体の変更が**不要**（`final: prev:` のまま）
- `_sources/` ディレクトリや `nvfetcher.toml` などの追加ファイルが**不要**
- vendorHash、cargoHash、npmDepsHash、pnpmDepsHash を**全て自動更新**
- flake.nix に `packages` 出力を追加するだけで済む

コミットして PR を作成しますか？

> DEVELOPER

依存も更新してくれるんじゃないの？PR自動マージしたいんだけど goがエラーになってるじゃん

> AGENT

nix-update は依存ハッシュを自動更新します。ただし仕組みは「ハッシュを空にする → ビルドする → エラーから正しいハッシュを抽出する」というものなので、ビルド自体が別の理由で失敗すると（aqua の場合は nixpkgs の Go が古い）ハッシュも取得できず丸ごと失敗します。

問題は、失敗時に nix-update がファイルを中途半端に書き換えたまま残すことです。次のパッケージに影響するのを防ぐ必要があります。

> TOOL

}

     2→
     3→on:
     4→  schedule:
     5→    - cron: "0 18 * * 5" # Saturday 03:00 JST
     6→  workflow_dispatch:
     7→
     8→permissions:
     9→  contents: write
    10→  pull-requests: write
    11→
    12→jobs:
    13→  update:
    14→    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm
    15→    steps:
    16→      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2
    17→        with:
    18→          persist-credentials: false
    19→
    20→      - id: setup-bot
    21→        uses: ./.github/actions/setup-git-bot
    22→        with:
    23→          app-id: ${{ secrets.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_ID }}
    24→          app-private-key: ${{ secrets.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}
    25→
    26→      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2
    27→        with:
    28→          token: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}
    29→
    30→      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-nix
    31→        with:
    32→          cachix-auth-token: ${{ secrets.CACHIX_AUTH_TOKEN }}
    33→
    34→      - name: Update overlay packages
    35→        run: |
    36→          nix shell nixpkgs#nix-update -c bash <<'SCRIPT'
    37→          set -uo pipefail
    38→
    39→          for pkg in aqua difit entire jj-desc keifu similarity-ts; do
    40→            echo "::group::Updating $pkg"
    41→            nix-update "$pkg" --flake --commit || echo "::warning::Failed to update $pkg"
    42→            echo "::endgroup::"
    43→          done
    44→
    45→          for pkg in polycat pretty-ts-errors-markdown; do
    46→            echo "::group::Updating $pkg"
    47→            nix-update "$pkg" --flake --version=branch --commit || echo "::warning::Failed to update $pkg"
    48→            echo "::endgroup::"
    49→          done
    50→          SCRIPT
    51→
    52→      - name: Format
    53→        run: nix fmt
    54→
    55→      - name: Commit formatting fixes
    56→        run: |
    57→          if ! git diff --quiet; then
    58→            git add -A
    59→            git commit -m "style: format overlay files"
    60→          fi
    61→
    62→      - name: Create pull request
    63→        env:
    64→          GH_TOKEN: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}
    65→        run: |
    66→          if [ "$(git rev-list --count origin/main..HEAD)" -eq 0 ]; then
    67→            echo "No overlay updates found"
    68→            exit 0
    69→          fi
    70→
    71→          branch="update-overlays"
    72→          git push --force origin "HEAD:refs/heads/$branch"
    73→
    74→          commits=$(git log --oneline origin/main..HEAD | sed 's/^/- /')
    75→          body="Automated update of custom overlay packages via \`nix-update\`.
    76→
    77→          ## Commits
    78→
    79→          ${commits}
    80→
    81→          ---
    82→          This PR was automatically created by the overlay update workflow."
    83→
    84→          pr_number=$(gh pr list --head "$branch" --json number --jq '.[0].number // empty')
    85→
    86→          if [ -n "$pr_number" ]; then
    87→            gh pr edit "$pr_number" --title "nix/overlays: update packages" --body "$body"
    88→          else
    89→            gh pr create \
    90→              --title "nix/overlays: update packages" \
    91→              --body "$body" \
    92→              --base main \
    93→              --head "$branch" \
    94→              --label dependencies --label automated
    95→
    96→            pr_number=$(gh pr list --head "$branch" --json number --jq '.[0].number')
    97→          fi
    98→
    99→          gh pr merge "$pr_number" --auto --squash || echo "Note: Auto-merge may require branch protection rules"
   100→

> AGENT

整理すると:

- nix-update は依存ハッシュ（vendorHash, cargoHash, npmDepsHash, pnpmDepsHash）を**自動更新する**
- 仕組みは「空ハッシュでビルド → エラーから正しいハッシュを抽出」
- aqua のケースは、nixpkgs の Go (1.25.5) が新版 aqua の要求 (>= 1.25.6) を満たせないためビルド自体が失敗 → ハッシュ抽出もできない
- これは nixpkgs が Go を更新すれば自然に解決する
- 失敗したパッケージは `git checkout -- nix/overlays/` でファイルを復元し、コミットされない
- PR には**成功したパッケージの更新だけ**が含まれるので、自動マージは安全

  "old_string": "      - name: Update overlay packages\n        run: |\n          nix shell nixpkgs#nix-update -c bash <<'SCRIPT'\n          set -uo pipefail\n\n          update_pkg() {\n            local pkg=\"$1\"\n            shift\n            echo \"::group::Updating $pkg\"\n            if nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --commit \"$@\"; then\n              echo \"Successfully updated $pkg\"\n            else\n              echo \"::warning::Failed to update $pkg, restoring files\"\n              git checkout -- nix/overlays/\n            fi\n            echo \"::endgroup::\"\n          }\n\n          for pkg in aqua difit entire jj-desc keifu similarity-ts; do\n            update_pkg \"$pkg\"\n          done\n\n          for pkg in polycat pretty-ts-errors-markdown; do\n            update_pkg \"$pkg\" --version=branch\n          done\n          SCRIPT",

> AGENT

これで:

1. パッケージ一覧は `nix eval .#packages.x86_64-linux` から動的に取得
2. まず通常の `nix-update`（タグベース）を試行
3. 失敗したらファイル復元して `--version=branch`（ブランチ追跡）にフォールバック
4. それも失敗したら復元して次へ

`flake.nix` の `packages` に追加すればワークフロー側の変更は不要です。

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/.github/workflows/update-overlays.yaml",
  "old_string": "      - name: Update overlay packages\n        run: |\n          nix shell nixpkgs#nix-update nixpkgs#jq -c bash <<'SCRIPT'\n          set -uo pipefail\n\n          packages=$(nix eval .#packages.x86_64-linux --apply 'pkgs: builtins.attrNames pkgs' --json | jq -r '.[]')\n\n          for pkg in $packages; do\n            echo \"::group::Updating $pkg\"\n            if nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --commit; then\n              echo \"Successfully updated $pkg\"\n            else\n              git checkout -- nix/overlays/\n              if nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --version=branch --commit; then\n                echo \"Successfully updated $pkg (branch tracking)\"\n              else\n                echo \"::warning::Failed to update $pkg, restoring files\"\n                git checkout -- nix/overlays/\n              fi\n            fi\n            echo \"::endgroup::\"\n          done\n          SCRIPT",
  "new_string": "      - name: Update overlay packages\n        run: |\n          nix shell nixpkgs#nix-update nixpkgs#jq -c bash <<'SCRIPT'\n          set -uo pipefail\n\n          packages=$(nix eval .#packages.x86_64-linux --apply 'pkgs: builtins.attrNames pkgs' --json | jq -r '.[]')\n          failed=\"\"\n\n          for pkg in $packages; do\n            echo \"::group::Updating $pkg\"\n            if nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --commit 2>&1; then\n              echo \"Successfully updated $pkg\"\n            else\n              git checkout -- nix/overlays/\n              if nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --version=branch --commit 2>&1; then\n                echo \"Successfully updated $pkg (branch tracking)\"\n              else\n                echo \"::warning::Failed to update $pkg, restoring files\"\n                git checkout -- nix/overlays/\n                failed=\"${failed}${failed:+,}${pkg}\"\n              fi\n            fi\n            echo \"::endgroup::\"\n          done\n\n          echo \"failed=$failed\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          SCRIPT"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/.github/workflows/update-overlays.yaml",
  "old_string": "          failed=\"\"\n\n          for pkg in $packages; do\n            echo \"::group::Updating $pkg\"\n            if nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --commit 2>&1; then\n              echo \"Successfully updated $pkg\"\n            else\n              git checkout -- nix/overlays/\n              if nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --version=branch --commit 2>&1; then\n                echo \"Successfully updated $pkg (branch tracking)\"\n              else\n                echo \"::warning::Failed to update $pkg, restoring files\"\n                git checkout -- nix/overlays/\n                failed=\"${failed}${failed:+,}${pkg}\"\n              fi\n            fi\n            echo \"::endgroup::\"\n          done\n\n          echo \"failed=$failed\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          SCRIPT",
  "new_string": "          : > /tmp/nix-update-failures\n\n          for pkg in $packages; do\n            echo \"::group::Updating $pkg\"\n            if nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --commit 2>&1; then\n              echo \"Successfully updated $pkg\"\n            else\n              git checkout -- nix/overlays/\n              if nix-update \"$pkg\" --flake --version=branch --commit 2>&1; then\n                echo \"Successfully updated $pkg (branch tracking)\"\n              else\n                echo \"::warning::Failed to update $pkg, restoring files\"\n                git checkout -- nix/overlays/\n                echo \"$pkg\" >> /tmp/nix-update-failures\n              fi\n            fi\n            echo \"::endgroup::\"\n          done\n          SCRIPT"
}

{

  "old_string": "      - name: Create pull request\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}\n        run: |\n          if [ \"$(git rev-list --count origin/main..HEAD)\" -eq 0 ]; then\n            echo \"No overlay updates found\"\n            exit 0\n          fi\n\n          branch=\"update-overlays\"\n          git push --force origin \"HEAD:refs/heads/$branch\"\n\n          commits=$(git log --oneline origin/main..HEAD | sed 's/^/- /')\n          body=\"Automated update of custom overlay packages via \\`nix-update\\`.\n\n          ## Commits\n\n          ${commits}\n\n          ---\n          This PR was automatically created by the overlay update workflow.\"\n\n          pr_number=$(gh pr list --head \"$branch\" --json number --jq '.[0].number // empty')\n\n          if [ -n \"$pr_number\" ]; then\n            gh pr edit \"$pr_number\" --title \"nix/overlays: update packages\" --body \"$body\"\n          else\n            gh pr create \\\n              --title \"nix/overlays: update packages\" \\\n              --body \"$body\" \\\n              --base main \\\n              --head \"$branch\" \\\n              --label dependencies --label automated\n\n            pr_number=$(gh pr list --head \"$branch\" --json number --jq '.[0].number')\n          fi\n\n          gh pr merge \"$pr_number\" --auto --squash || echo \"Note: Auto-merge may require branch protection rules\"",
  "new_string": "      - name: Create pull request\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}\n        run: |\n          failures=\"\"\n          if [ -s /tmp/nix-update-failures ]; then\n            failures=$(sed 's/^/- `/' /tmp/nix-update-failures | sed 's/$/`/')\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"$(git rev-list --count origin/main..HEAD)\" -eq 0 ]; then\n            echo \"No overlay updates found\"\n            exit 0\n          fi\n\n          branch=\"update-overlays\"\n          git push --force origin \"HEAD:refs/heads/$branch\"\n\n          commits=$(git log --oneline origin/main..HEAD | sed 's/^/- /')\n\n          body=\"Automated update of custom overlay packages via \\`nix-update\\`.\n\n          ## Commits\n\n          ${commits}\"\n\n          if [ -n \"$failures\" ]; then\n            body=\"${body}\n\n          ## Failed to update\n\n          ${failures}\"\n          fi\n\n          body=\"${body}\n\n          ---\n          This PR was automatically created by the overlay update workflow.\"\n\n          pr_number=$(gh pr list --head \"$branch\" --json number --jq '.[0].number // empty')\n\n          if [ -n \"$pr_number\" ]; then\n            gh pr edit \"$pr_number\" --title \"nix/overlays: update packages\" --body \"$body\"\n          else\n            gh pr create \\\n              --title \"nix/overlays: update packages\" \\\n              --body \"$body\" \\\n              --base main \\\n              --head \"$branch\" \\\n              --label dependencies --label automated\n\n            pr_number=$(gh pr list --head \"$branch\" --json number --jq '.[0].number')\n          fi\n\n          gh pr merge \"$pr_number\" --auto --squash || echo \"Note: Auto-merge may require branch protection rules\""
}

変更内容:

- **aqua overlay 削除**: Go バージョン非互換のため
- **`nix/overlays/default.nix`**, **`nix/modules/home/packages.nix`**: aqua 参照を除去
